#EN Objective: Take control of a hero in search of the hidden truth.  Complete quests, acquire powerful guardians, and slash through monsters as you explore the world of Soltia.\n\n \n\n\nMove: Navigation Keys, 2, 4, 6, 8;Select/Search/Talk: Center Nav Key, 5;Standard attack: Center Nav Key, 5;Strong attack: 7;Guardian skill: 1, 3;Potion: 9;Change potion: #;Mini-map: 0;Menu/Pause: CLR, Right Soft key; Combo: Combine attacks to execute a combo-attack. For example, press 5, 5, 5 or 5, 5, 5, 7. Completing certain sub-quests will increase the number of combos you can do. Note that if your SP gauge is zero, then you cannot execute combo moves. \n\nOptions: \n\nSound: Adjust sound.\nGame: Adjust game speed.\nSpeech: Adjust speed character's speech appears. \nCamera: Alters position of the camera. On = more screen is shown in front of the main character. Off = character’s default position is always center of the screen.\n\nNew Game: Start from the beginning.\nLoad Game: Start from a previously saved point.\nSave: Save game progress.\nHelp: Instructions on how to use and play the game.\nAbout: Information on the developer, licensor, publisher, support and version number.\nExit: Exit the game.

#EN Each Guardian allows you to use different magic and also bestows on you the particular element of that Guardian (e.g. your character will take on the attribute of fire if you equip a fire Guardian). Magical skills can be assigned to shortcut keys.; There are 3 elements - fire, water, and earth. The level of damage depends on the elemental attribute of the Guardian equipped, so try switching Guardians if it is difficult to defeat an enemy.; The equipped Guardian gains experience along with the main character, which will also unlock new Guardian skills.; You can select 2 Guardians when you start a game. During the game, you can acquire other Guardians through quests.

#EN Each time you level up, you'll receive 3 points to spend on your abilities.; You can upgrade the following: power (attack strength), HP (HP amount and its recovery speed), agility (strike accuracy, avoiding attacks, critical attack percentages), and will (SP and its recovery speed).; HP and SP recover slowly when not in battle. SP is consumed when you make an attack.
1236	За каждый новый уровень вы получаете 3 очка для улучшения способностей.; Можно улучшать: Силу (мощь атаки), Здоровье (запас и скорость восстановления), Ловкость (точность, уклонение, шанс критического удара) и Волю (запас энергии и скорость ее восстановления).; Вне боя здоровье и энергия медленно восстанавливаются. Каждая атака тратит энергию.
